===== INTERPRETATION LIMITS =====
1. Address construction does not prove the data meaning.
2. A pointer may refer to a runtime object, not a static ELF table.
3. LUI+ADDIU/ORI scan is a candidate scan, not full dataflow analysis.
4. GP-relative values may be state flags, counters, or pointers.
5. ID 40-44 arguments do not prove weapon attack power.
6. No runtime memory was accessed.
7. No game data or ELF data was modified.
8. Do not implement memory writes from these candidates alone.
